Police, Kanjirappally to be acting Circle Inspector of 
Police, s . B. C. I. D. with effect from 6-4-1955 vice 
Sri V. Narayana Iyer proceeded on leave with effect from 
6-4-1955 F. N.

Police, Thuckalai to be acting Circle Inspector of Police 
Vaikom with effect from 11-4-1955 F. N. 

4. Sri P. N. Balakrishna Pillai, acting Circle Inspector 
of Police on return from leave to be acting Circle 
Inspector of Police, Thuckalai with effect from 2-4-1955 
F. N.

holding independent charge of the duties of the Circle

from 2-4-1955 F. N.

pally with effect from 2-4-1955 F. N.

Insert the following after the heading " Miscellaneous 
Rulings particulars" . 

"General- The term , Government referred to in the 
Appendix shall be construed as Government in the 
Administrative Department except in the following 
cases : 
(1) Expenditure which exceeds Rs. 250 except where 

higher powers have been delegated in excess 

of this amount; 
(ii) expenditure of a recurring nature: 
(iii) expenditure which has not been provided for 

in the budget : 
(iv ) cases where relaxation of any financial rule is 

necessary : 
( v ) Special cases. 
(Circular No. Fin . (b ) 4-2651353 dated 25th May 1954)

NOTIFICATION 
R. 11 (3)-1320/55. 

17th May 1955. 
Candidates intending to apply or who have applied for 
selection for recruitment to the post of Statistical Officer on 
Rs. 70-150 in response to this office notification published as 
item No. I in the Gazette dated 10-5-1955 are hereby informed 
that the number of vacancies of Statistical Officers has been 
reduced to two from four as notified . 


LATE NOTIFICATION 
No. LS. 1-2341255/DD . 

16th May 1955. 
The following craft of certain further amendment 
to the Travancore-Cochin Minimum Wages Rules, 1951 
which Government propose to make in exercise of the 
powers conferred by Clause ( 11) of the proviso to Section 
15 and Clause (h ) of sub-section (2 ) of Section 30 of 
the Minimum Wages Act, 1948 (Central Act XI of 1948 ) 
is hereby published as required by sub-section ( 1) of 
Section 30 of the said Act for the information of all 
persons likely to be affected thereby and notice is hereby 
given that the draft will be taken into consideration on 
or after the 18th June 1955. 
Any objection or suggestion which may be received 

to the said draft before the date specified 

DRAFT AMENDMENT. 
In the said Rules, after Rule 21, the following shall 
be inserted as Rule 21- A , namely : 

21- A . If an employee is engaged in a work of such 
a nature that the duration of the work per day will not 
ordinarily extend to the duration prescribed under these 
Rules for a normal working day, the wages that shall 
be payable to such an employee shall be in the propor 
tion which the number of hours he is actually working 
hou prescribed for normal working 

By order of His Highness 

the Raj Pramukh , 
Government Secretariat, K. NARAYANA MENON , 

L. S. G. (Municipal) Addl. Secretary to Government.

Notifications by Heads of Departments . 
DEPARTMENT OF ANIMAL HUSBANDRY 

ത്തിലേയ്ക്കു 1955 ൽ ബർമാസം ഒന്നാംതീയതി മുതൽ 1956 

മാർച്ചുമാസം 31 - com- വരെയുള്ള തീ g റി സാധനങ്ങൾ 
1955 മേയ് 9. 

- പ്രസ്തുത നെല്ലിയാംപതി കാഫ്” റെയറിംഗ സ്റ്റേഷനിൽ 
ആനിമൽ ഹസ്ബൻഡ്ര ഡിപ്പാർട്ടുമെന്നും ൻറ എത്തിച്ചു തുക്കം തരുന്നത്. ലയ ള്ള കൺട്രാക്റമ പിടി 
അധീനതയിൽ നെല്ലിയാംപതി കാഡ്റെയറിംഗ് സ്റ്റേ ക്കണമെന്നാഗ്രഹിക്കുന്നവർ ാവരു ടെ ടെൻഡറക 
ഷനിൽ സംരക്ഷിച്ചുവരുന്ന കന്നുകാലികളുടെ ആവശ്യ നെന്മാറ വേററിനറി സർജൻറ പേര്1955 മേയ

23 -ാം നു തിങ്കളാ , നാലുമണി ക്കു മുമ്പു കിട്ടത്തക്കവണ്ണം 
അയച്ചുതരേണ്ടതാ ണു . ടെൻഡറിൽ ടെൻഡർ അ യ യ്ക്കു ന്ന 
ആളിന്റെ പൂർണ്ണമായ മേൻ വ ലാസം കാണിച്ചിരിക്കേ 
ണ്ടതും പ്രിട്ടീഷ് പൗണ്ടുതുങ്ങാത്തി നു ര. വൈ 
തീ ooീസാധനങ്ങൾ ഇന്നവിലയ്ക്കു ത വര കൊള്ളാമെന്നു 
വ്യക്തമായി കാണിച്ചിരിക്കേണ്ടതുമാണു . ടെൻഡർ അയ 
യ ന്നതി നു ആഗ്രഹിക്കുന്ന ആൾ 20 രൂപ അഡ്വാൻ 
സാ യ നെയാ? വെറ്ററിനറി സർലൻറ പേ 
രസീതു മേടിച്ചുകൊള്ളനടതും, 

ടെൻഡർ 
അടക്കം ചെയ്തയയ്ക്കുന്ന കവർ മുദ്രവച്ച് ഇന്നതിലേയ്ക്കു ള്ള 
ടെൻഡ ർ എന്നുകവറ: ൻ പുറത്തു എഴുതിയിരിക്കേണ്ട 
മാകുന്നു. 

1955 മേയ്23-ാം- നാലു മണിക്കുശേഷം കിട്ടുന്ന 
ടെൻഡറുകൾ സ്വീകരിക്കുന്നതല്ല. എ co വും . കറണ ത 
റോം നുള്ള ടെൻറർ അംഗീകൾ ക്കുന്നതും ആ യ തില 
യം ഉള്ള അധികാരം ആനിമൽ ഹസ്ബ ൻ ഡ്രി ഡയറക്ടർ 
അ വ ർകളിൽ നിക്ഷിപ്തമായിരിക്കുന്നതുമാകുന്നു. ഏതെ 
ങ്കിലും ടെൻഡർ അംഗീകരിച്ചു ക ഴി ഞ്ഞ ൽ ഉടൻതന്നെ 
വ പ രം ടെൻഡർ അയച്ച ആളിനെ തെയ്യപ്പെടുത്തുന്ന തും 
വിവരം തെയ്യപ്പെടുത്തുന്ന തീയതി മുതൽ 7 ദിവസത്തി 
നകം 50 രൂപാ സെക്യറ്ററിയായി കെട്ടിവച്ച ഒരു രൂപ 
മുദ്രപ്പത്രത്തിൽ ക 411 ട്രാക്റ്റ് ശരിയാംവണ്ണം നടത്തി 
ക്കൊള്ളമമെന്നുള്ള ഉടമ്പടി എഴുതി വയ്ക്കു ണ്ട തുമാകുന്നു. 
ഉടമ്പടി എഴുതിവച്ചുകഴി ഞ്ഞ ൽ വെറ്ററിനറി സർജൻ 
ആവശ്യപ്പെടുന്ന തീയതി മുതൽ സാധനങ്ങൾ എലി ച 
തുടങ്ങുകയും, എല്ലിക്ക, ന്ന സാധനങ്ങൾ കല്ല്, കരടു മുത 
ലായവ കലപില്ലാതിരിക്കുന്നവയായിരിക്കുകയുംചെയ്യേ 

ണ്ടതാണു". മോശമെന്നു തോന്നുന്ന സാധനങ്ങൾ എടു 
ക്കാതെ മടക്കി അയയ്ക്കു ന്നപക്ഷം പകരം നല്ല സാധ 
നങ്ങൾ കൊണ്ടുവന്നേല്പിക്കേണ്ടതാകുന്നു.. സാധനങ്ങ4 d 

ററിനറി സർജൻ ഇൻഡൻറിൻപ്രകാരം കൃത്യസ 
മയങ്ങളിൽതന്നെ എത്തിച്ചുകൊള്ളണ്ട തും, ആയതിൽ 


വരുന്ന വീ ഴ യ ് ക ൺ ട്രാക്ടർ ഉത്തര വാദിയായിരി ക്കുന്നതു 
മാകുന്നു. പരസ്യത്ത് ൽ കാണിച്ച്ഉള്ള സാധനങ്ങളുടെ 
ക ണ ക ഉദ്ദേ ശ മ യ ട്ടുള്ള തും കൂടു ര ലായി ആവശ്യപ്പെ 
ട്ടാൽ അ തനുസരിച്ചു ഏല്പിക്കേണ്ട തും ത ത ല്ലം കുറവായി 
ആവശ്യപ്പെടുന്നപക്ഷം മുഴുവനും എടുക. ണ മെന്നു ശഠി 
ക്കുവാൻ പാടില്ലാത്തതുമാകുന്നു. വശ്യമു ള്ള എ മാത 
ങ്കി ലും സാധനങ്ങൾ എല്ല " ക്കു തിരി ക്കുന്നപക്ഷം വേറെ 
ടെൻഡർ വിളിച്ചോ, മാം വിധത്തിലോ വാങ്ങുന്നതും 
ആയതിന്മേലുണ്ടാകുന്ന നഷ്ടത" നു കൺട്രാക്ടർ ഉത്തരവാ 
ദിയായിരിക്കുന്നതും, അ തല്ലാലാഭമാണുണം കുന്നതെങ്കിൽ 
ആയതു കൺട്രാക്ട് അവകാശപ്പെടാൻ പാടില്ലാത്ത തു 
മാകുന്നു. പ്രസ്തുത വ്യവസ്ഥ കൾ അനുസരിച്ചു കൺട്രോ 
ക്റം നടത്തുന്നതിൽ ക ൺട്രാക്ട് ഏററിട്ടുള്ളവർ ഏതെ 
ങ്കിലും വില്ല, വരുത്തുന്നപക്ഷം ആയതി ൽ ന " ന്നുണ്ടാകു ന്ന 
സകല ന ഷ്ടങ്ങളും 

ട്യാൻറ സകരിയറി യിൽ

TRAVANCORE -CUCHIN GAZETTE 


REVIEW . 


2nd May 1955 . 


WORKING CLASS COST OF LIVING INDEX NUMBERS FOR THEMOFUSSIL CENTRES COMPRISING OLLUR, 
PUDUKAD AND CHALAKUDY AND FOR THE KARUVANNUR CENTRE FOR THE MONTI: 

OF MARCH 1955. 
Ollur, Pudukad and Chalakudy .---Index No. 371 - A fall of three points. 

The index for the food group receded bynine points to 416 as a result of a decline in the prices of tapioca, milk , ghee , 
Cocoanut oil, Tamarind,turmeric and jaggery .. 

The index for the miscellaneous group advanced by seventeen points to 344 chiefly due to a rise in the price of betel leaves. 

The indices in respect of fuel and lighting, clothing and house rent groups remained stationary at 354, 397 and 103 
respectively. 

Karuvannur.- Index No. 369 - Stationary. 

The index for the food group fell by 0.30 point to 415.14 as a consequence of a fall in the prices of cocoanut and milk . This 
fall has not, however, affected the cost of living index number . 

The indices in respect of Fuel and Lighting, Clothing, House- rent and miscellaneous groups remained unchanged at 346, 
400, 100 and 326 respectively .

There is provision for the following course also . 

M. Sc.- IN APPLIED CHEMISTRY. 
Candidates for admission to the M. Sc. Course 
in Applied Chemistry should have passed the B. Sc. 
Degree Examination of the Travancore University or 
a Degree in some other University accepted by the 
Syndicate as equivalent thereto with Chemistry as the 
main subject and at least one of the following subjects 
as subsidiary- Physics, Zoology and Botany. 

Facilities for Research , leading to higher Degrees 
in (a ) Pure Organic Chemistry and (b ) Physical and

( 2 ) 

2nd May 1955 . 
EXPORT QUALITY ROSEWOOD - AUCTION SALE 

AT THE CHALAKUDY DEPOT ON 30-5-1955 . 

Export Quality Rosewood of about 440 tons 
now in stock and further arrivals at the Chala 
kudy Depot till date auction will be sold in 
public auction at the Chalakudy Depot on 30-5-55 
commercing from 1 P. M. subject to the special 
conditions given below and the usual conditions 
of sale of timber at Government Timber Depots 
published atpages 270 and 271 Forest sheetof 
Gazette dated 28-9-1954 and page 349 of Gazette 
dated 30-11-1954 and as per rules in Appendix 
XXIV of the Travancore -Cochin Forest Code. 

2. Intending bidders will be required to 
furnish an earnest money deposit of Rs. 500 
without which they will not be allowed to bid. 


3. Each bidder will have to remit the 1/3 of 
the total bid amount including the earnest money 
deposit or Rs. 5000 whichever is less at the close of 
the auction failing which his earnest money 
deposit shall be forfeited to the Government and 
the timber resold at his risk and loss. 

4. The balance 2/3 value should be remitted 
within 30 days from the date of intimation of 
confirmation of the sale and in default, the 
amount already remitted will be forfeited to the 
Government and the timber resold at his risk and 
loss 

5. Amounts if any, due recovery from the 
defaulting purchasers will be treated as arrears 
of land revenue and recovered as per Revenue 
Recovery Act in force . 

6. The bidders should satisfy themselvesabout 
the quality and conditions of the logs prior to 
auction . The sale of the logs is as and where 
they are and complaints after the auction will 
not be taken notice of. 

7. If for any reason the auction of the export 
quality of Rosewood is over or not conducted on 
that day the usual auction of other species will be 
taken up that day and continued the next day 
viz . 31-5-1955. 


C. M. JOHN , 
DivisionalForest Officer . 


Chalakudy.

കല്ലൂർ വില്ലേജിൽ മുള്ള ക്കര പൊതു ദേവസ്സി 
യുടെ പുകയിലപ്പീടികയുടെമുൻവശമുള്ള പബ്ല്. ആ റോ 

ഡിൽ ന് ന്നും 28-2-55- ൽ ചെറുവാൽക്കാരൻ അ 
പരസ്യം. 

ന്തോ ണ്.മകൾ മതി എന്ന കട്ടി ക്ക് കിട്ടിയതും ഉടമസ്ഥ 

നാരാണെന്നു അറിയാം 10 തിനാൽ അളഗപ്പനഗർ പോ 
1955 മേയം. 2 . 

ലീസ് സ്റ്റേഷനിൽ ഹാജരാക്കിയതും ഇക്കാൾ ഈ കോ 
കൊച്ചി രണ്ടാംക്ലാസ്സ് മജിസ്ട്രേട്ട് കോടതിയിൽ ട്ടിൽ ഇരിപ്പുള്ളതും താഴെ വിവരം പറയുന്നതുമായ മുത ലു 

കൾക്കു നേരായ ഉടമസ്ഥ അവ ങ്കി ൽ ഈ പരസ്യത്തി 
അ റി വാനം യി പരസ്യം25 
നിന്നും. എ ല്ലാവരും 

യത് മുതൽ മൂന്നുമാസ ത്തിനുള്ള ൽ ഈ കോർട്ടിൽ വാജ 
ന്നതു്. 

രായി മടക്കിവാങ്ങാത്തപക്ഷം ടി മു ത ലുകൾ ലേലത്തി 
2.3.1955- ൽ രാവിലെ 9 മണീ ” കൊച്ചീ ക്കായ 

വിറം സക്കാര് ലേയ്ക്കു മുതൽ കൂട്ടുന്നതാ ണെ ന്നു ” ഇതിനാൽ 
വിൽ പൊന്തിക്കിടന്നിരുന്നതും കൊച്ചി മട്ടാഞ്ചേരി പരസ്യപ്പെടുത്തുന്നു.

NOTICE . 


No. wc. 53355 . 

4th April 1955 . 
Whereas the Executive Engineer, Hydro 
Electric Project, Poringalkuthu has deposited 
with me Rs. 1800 (One thousand eight hundred ) 
only under the Workmen s Compensation Act 
(Central Act No. VIII of 1923) towards compen 
sation for distribution to the dependants of de 
ceased Sanku Nair, a workman employed in the 
Hydro-Electric Project, Poringalkuthu and where 
as it is reported that his wife Karthiyani Amma 
and minor unmarried daughter Lakshmikutty 
who is under the guardianship of the mother, the 
said Karthiyani Amma residing at Vellaikkal, 
Kizhakkumpattukara. Trichur are hist only 
dependants (as defined below ) entitled to the 
said compensation , any other persons claiming to 
be dependants of the deceased, should within 
15 days of the publication of this notice in the 
Government Gazette prefer their claims before 
me in writing and prove the same. If no such 
claims are preferred , the compensation will be 
disbursed to the above dependants. Claims or 
objections received thereafter will not be con 
sidered. 

" "Dependant means any of the following 
relatives of a deceased workman , namely : 

(i) a widow , a minor legitimate son , and un 
married legitimate daughter, or a widowed 
: mother ; and 

(ii) if wholly or in part dependent on the 
earnings of the workman at the time of his death , 
a widower, a parent other than a widowed mother, 


a minor illegitimate son , an unmarried illegiti 
mate daughter, a daughter legitimate or illegiti 
mate if married and a minor or if widowed, a 
minor brother , an unmarried or widowed sister, a 
widowed daughter-in -law , a minor child of a de 
ceased son, a minor child of a deceased daughter 
where no parent of the child is alive, or, where 
no parent of the workman is alive, a paternal 
grand-parent.

PART II 
MEDICAL AND PUBLIC HEALTH DEPARTMENTS 


GOVERNMENT AYURVEDA COLLEGE, 

TRIVANDRUM . 
PROSPECTUS FOR 1955 to 1956 . 
The College will be reopened after the Mid 
summer vacation on Monday the 4th July 1955. 

The prospectus both for the Degree and 
Diploma Courses in Ayurveda instituted in this 
College, under the Faculty of Ayurveda by the 
University of Travancore are given below . 

1. Degree course in Ayurveda (B.A.M.). 
Instituted in 1952. 50 candidates will be admitted 
to this course . 

(i) Eligibility for admission : -Those who 
have passed the Intermediate Examination the 
Travancore University with Biology, Physics and 
Chemistry or an examination recognised by the 
Syndicate as equivalent thereto and those who 
possess knowledge of Sanskrit will be eligible for 
admission to this course. 

(ii) Age : -The applicants should have com 
pleted the age of 17 years on or before the 30th 
June 1955 or will complete this age within four 
months of that date . 

(iii) Duration :-The Degree course shall be 
for a period of five years plus one year s intern 
ship in the College hospital and in other Govern 
ment hospitals of the State . 

The course shall impart thorough theoretical 
and practical instructions in the various branches 
of Ayurveda which will be supplemented with 
detailed study in Anatomy and Physiology to the 
same standard as for the Degree course in Modern 
Medicine and instructions in the general princi 
ples of other subjects such as Pathology , Bacterio 
logy Medicine, Surgery, Midwifery and Gynae 
cology, Medical Jurisprudence, Toxicology and 
Hygiene. 

Degree shallbe awarded only on production 
of certificates of having conducted at least ten 
normal labour cases (Prasuthikarma and Salya 
Salakya) ( 2 ) of having assisted in the conduct of 


at least ten autopsies as a post mortem clerk , and 
(3) of having undergone a course of surgical 
dressership for a period of ninemonths. 

(iv) Tuition fee : -Annual fees for the Degree 
course is Rs. 160 (One hundred and sixty) payable 
in 8 equal monthly instalments . 

(v ) Application fee : -A fee of Rs. 5 should 
be paid along with the application for admission 
to the course. This application fee will not be 
refunded in any case . 

2. Diploma course in Ayurveda (D. A. M.). 
Instituted in 1951. 75 candidates will be admitted 
to this course . 

(i) Eligibility for admission : -Those who 
have passed Secondary SchoolLeaving Certi 
ficate examination conducted by the Travancore 
Cochin Government, or an examination accepted 
by theSyndicateasequivalent thereto and possess 
knowledge of Sanskrit will be eligible for admis 
sion to the course . 

(ii) Age : - Applicants should have attained 
the age of 16 on or before the date of admission 
to the course. 

(iii) Duration :-The Diploma course shall 
be for a period of 4 years plus nine months 
apprenticeship in the college hopstital. The 
diploma certificate will be awarded only after the 
apprenticeship and on production of evidence of 
having conducted ten normal labour cases ( Pra 
suthikarma) and Salya Salakya . 

(iv ) Tuition fee :-- Annual fees for the course 
will be Rs. 96 (Ninety six ) payable by 8 monthly 
instalments . 

(v ) Application fee :-A fee of Rs. 2 should 
be paid along with the application for admission 
to the course. This application fee will not be 
refunded in any case.

students from other Universities.

5. Default of fees : -If any students of the 
College fails to pay the fees (or special fees) on 
the due date he/she is liable to pay a fine of four(4) 
annas. If he/she fails to pay the fees and fines on 
or before the tenth day after the due date , he/she 
will not be permitted to attend the College , and 
shall be permitted to renew his /her attendance 
only on payment of the fee and fine as shown 
above together with a further fine of Re. 1. 

Students who have not been permitted to 
attend the College on account of non -payment of 
fees will also be removed from the rolls after such 
loss of attendance for 15 consecutive working 
days . They may be re -admitted only with the 
special permission of the Principal and on pay 
ment of a re -admission fee of Rs. 2. Students will 
pay particular attention to the defaulter s list that 
will be published every month . 

6. Date of joining : -Selected candidates 
should join the College on the date of reopening.

mentmanagement. The owners can exercise their

TRAVANCORE -COCHIN GAZETTE 


( PART III 


C. ROC . 2647 |CR |54 . 
Proceedings of the Regional Transport Board , 

Trichuz, dated 13-1-55 . 
ITEM No. XXVI- 7 . 
Read : -1. Report dated 8-10-54 of the Traffic 

Sub-Inspector, Trichur wherein it is 
stated that he checked bus TCC . 435 
at 6-55 P. M. on 4-10-54 at Viyyur and 
found that the bus started from the 
bus stand at 6-20 P. M. tarried enroute 
to canvas passengers and took 35 
minutes to cover a distance of 11 
miles and that there were 42 persons 
in the bus as against the sanctioned 35 . 
The permit holder of the bus is Ms. 
Karippala Motor Service and the 
Driver was Sri K.Prabhakara Menon , 
Kallazhi House , Cheruthuruthy, 

holder of M. D.L C. 2742. 
2. The explanation of the owner dated 

28-10-54. 
3. The explanation of the driver and 
conductor dated 28-10-54. 

ORDER. 
The driver and conductor admit the offence 
of over- load and also that the vehicle was late , 
but explained that thelatter was due to a punctured 
tyre. The explanations are not satisfactory. The 
offences are proved. The permit ofbus TCC . 435 
is suspended for 3 weeks under Section 60 of the 
Motor Vehicles Act 1939 for overload and com 
petency certificate of the driver Sri K. Prabha 
kara Menon is suspended for 1 month under Sec 
tion 16 of the Motor Vehicles Act 1939 for tarry 
ing the bus on 4-10-54 . The suspension will take 
effect from the date of surrender of the records . 

P. A.MOHAMED, 
Trichur. 

Secretary , R.T.B.

should be for delivery of the articles at the places 
mentioned below the schedule . The necessary superscrip . 
tion , the duo date for the receipt of quotations and 
the date up to which the rates will have to remain 
without superscription or received after the guetation 
and time is liable to be rejected. The maximum period 
required for delivery of the articles should also be 
mentioned . 

The acceptance of the quotation will be subject to 
the following conditions : 

( 1 ) The successful tenderer must within a fortnight 
A month after the acceptance of his quotation furnish 
5 per cent of the amount of the contract as security 
deposit and execute an agreement at his own cost for 
the satisfactory fulfilment of the contract, if so required. 

( 2 ) Withdrawal from the quotation after it is 
accepted or failure to supply within a specified time or 
according to specifications will entail cancellation of the 
order and purchases being made at the offerer s expense 
from elsewhere, any loss incurred thereby being payable 
by the defaulting party. In such an event the Govern 
ment reserve also the right to remove his name from 
the list of Government suppliers permanently or for N 
specified number of years. 

( 3 ) Samples, duly listed, should be forwarded where 
ever possible under separate cover and the unapproved 
samples got back as early as possible by the offerers 
at their own expenses and the Government will in no 
case be liable for any expense on account of the value 
to the samples or their transport charges etc. In case 
the samples are sent by railway, the railway receipt 
should be sent separately, and not along with the 
quotation , since the quotation will be opened only on 
the appointed day and demurrage will have to be paid 
if the railway parcels are not cleared in time. The 
approved samples may or may not be returned at the 
discretion of the Secretary, Stores Purchase Conmittee . 
Sample sent by V. P. Post or freight to pay vill not 
be accepted . 

( 4 ) No representation for enhancement of price once 
accepted will be considered during the currency of the 
contract. 

(5 ) Any attempt on the part of tenderers or their 
agents to influence the Stores Purchase Committee in 
their favour by personal canvassing, either with the 
members of the Committee or with the members who 
may be co -opted , will disqualify the tenderers . 

(6 ) If any licence or permit is required, tenderers 
must specify it in their quotation and also state the 
authority to whom application is to be made. 

( 7 ) The quotation may be for the entire or part sup 
ples. But the tenderers should be prepared to carry out 
such portion of the supplies included in their quotation as 
may be allotted to them . 

( 8 ) In the absence of specific stipulation to the con 
trary in the quotations, it will be presumed that the 
prices quoted are inclusive of all taxes . 

( 9 ) Samples sent by post, railway or plane should 
have been despatched before the due date of the quota 
tion and should be received in this Oflice not later than 
three days from the due date of the quotation . Samples 
from local tenderers should be delivered before the due 
date and time of the tender. Quotations in respect of 
which samples are received late are liable to rejection 

(10 ) Telegraphic quotations will not bo considered 
unless they are immediately followed by confirmation 
with full relevant details posted before the due date of 
the quotation

18. Every notice hereby required or authorised to 
be given may be either given to the contractor personally 
or left at his residence or last known place of abode or 
business, or may be handed over to his agent personally, 
or may be addressed to the contractor by Post at his 
Jisual or last known place of abode or business and if 
so addressed and posted, shall be deemed to have been 
served on the contractor on the date on which , in the 
ordinary course of post a letter so addressed and posted 
would reach his place of abode or business . 

19. The tenderer shall undertake to supply materials 
according to the standard samples and specifications. 

20. No representation for enhancement of rates once 
accepted will be considered . 

21. Any attempt on the part of the tenderers or 
their agents to influence the Committee in their favour 
by personal canvassing either with the members of the 
Committee or with the members who may be co-opted 
will disqualify the tenderers . 

22. Tenderers should be prepared to accept orders 
subject to a penalty clause for forfeiture of security in 
the event of default in supplies or failure to supply with 
in the stipulated period. 

23. Samples sent by post, railway or plane should 
have been despatched before the due date of the tender 
and should be received in this office not later than three 
days from the due date of the tender. Samples from 
local tenderers should be delivered before the due date 
and time of the tender. Tenders from firms whose 
samples are received late except in accordance with the 
above provisions will not be considered on any account. 

21. Telegraphic quotations will not be considered un 
less immediately followed by confirmation with full 
relevant details posted before the due date of the tender . 

25. If insurance, sales tax or other local tax are to be 
paid extra , the fact must be clearly specified in the 
tenders. Otherwise it would be presumed that the tenders 
include all such charges.

Sri Ravi Varma Thampuran , Income-tax 
Officer (Salary Circle, Ernakulam , under orders 
of transfer as Income- tax Officer, Kottayam ) on 
joining duty atKottayam , is granted earned leave 
for 30 days from 16-5-1955 to 14-6-1955 (both days 
inclusive) . On the expiry of the leave he is likely 
to be re-posted to the same office.
